8749         function showToast(content, duration) {
877a             var toast = document.getElementById("Toast");
87b5             if (toast == null) {
87d7                 var toast = document.createElement("div");
8813                 toast.setAttribute("id", "Toast");
8847                 toast.setAttribute("class", "toast-layout");
8885                 document.body.appendChild(toast);
88b8                 showToast(content, duration);
88e7                 return;
8900             }
890f             
891d             var toastItem = document.createElement("div");
8959             toastItem.setAttribute("class", "toast");
8990             toastItem.innerHTML = '<div class="content">' + content + '</div>';
89e1             
89ef             setTimeout(function () {
8a15                 toastItem.setAttribute('class', 'toast hide-toast');
8a5b             }, duration);
8a76             
8a84             toastItem.onanimationend = function () {
8aba                 toastItem.remove();
8adf                 // 
8b0a toast
8b2d toast
8b3a                 if (toast.children.length === 0) {
8b6e                     toast.remove();
8b93                 }
8ba6             };
8bb6             
8bc4             toast.appendChild(toastItem);
8bef         }
